# Inventory: plan, requirements, DoD, gates, packages (read-only reader, 2026-10-19)

Scope read: handover/HANDOVER.md sections 2.4-4.9 + appendices; handover/REQUIREMENTS-STATUS-FINAL.md; handover/PACKAGES-AND-BRANCHES.md; design/MASTER-PLAN.md.
All paths are relative to D:/projects/tools/.overhaul/ . Format: `path:line`. Status is what the document states, with the document's own date. Nothing here is a judgment of whether it is really done.

Abbreviations: HO = handover/HANDOVER.md ; RSF = handover/REQUIREMENTS-STATUS-FINAL.md ; PAB = handover/PACKAGES-AND-BRANCHES.md ; MP = design/MASTER-PLAN.md
ID families: STATE-* (facts/capabilities in HO 2.x), GOAL-n (HO 3.1), REQ-n (HO 3.2), DOD-A.n / DOD-B.n (HO 3.3/3.4), GATE-<name> (HO 3.5 gates), NUM-<metric> (HO 3.5 phone numbers), PKG-<WP> (HO 4.2 table), DEP-n (HO 4.3), MERGE-n (HO 4.4), CP-n (checkpoints), PLAN-<WP>.n (HO 4.6 and plan steps), CRIT-n (HO 4.7), MERGEALL-n (HO 4.8), ETA-n (HO 4.9).

Default status date for HO rows: 2026-10-16 (HO "Oct 16, 11:40 IST" snapshot) unless the row says another date.

Parts in this file: 1 HO 2.4-2.10 (STATE-*); 2 HO 3.1 goals (GOAL-*); 3 HO 3.2 requirements (REQ-*); 4 HO 3.3/3.4 definitions of done (DOD-*); 5 HO 3.5 gates (GATE-*, NUM-*); 6 HO 4.1/4.2 (PLAN-4.1.*, PKG-*); 7 HO 4.3-4.5 (DEP-*, MERGE-*, CP-*); 8 HO 4.6 package plans (PLAN-<WP>.n); 9 HO 4.7-4.9 (CRIT-*, MERGEALL-*, ETA-*); 10 HO appendices A-D + REQ 39 addendum (APPX-*); 11 REQUIREMENTS-STATUS-FINAL (RSF-*, per requirement: status, .remains, .gate = acceptance check, plus RSF-9.n user decisions); 12 PACKAGES-AND-BRANCHES (PAB-*, MERGE-PAB.*, ETA-PAB); 13 MASTER-PLAN (MP-*, GATE-*.mp, MATRIX-*, OWN-*, RISK-*, RULE-*, PLAN-<WP>.mp<n> for B.4, PLAN-A*/PLAN-WP*.A* for Part A subsections); 14 differences between documents (DIFF-*); 15 what was not covered. MASTER-PLAN ids with ".mp" suffix duplicate a HANDOVER id on purpose (same gate or checkpoint seen from the other document).

## Part 13: design/MASTER-PLAN.md (MP)

IDs: MP-<section> for structure/decisions, PLAN-<WP>.<n> for package plan steps and acceptance items per MASTER-PLAN Part B/A (plan steps and acceptance), CP-n for checkpoints.

| id | what | source | owner | documented status (date) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| MP-0.status | MASTER-PLAN status: final design for implementation phase, revision 2 (after two independent critic reviews); every decision binding unless an implementation agent finds hard evidence (build failure, library behaviour, device measurement), then recorded in the handoff and the orchestrator decides; three decisions explicitly left to the user at checkpoints (motion feel, glass feel, release; B.5); inputs REQUIREMENTS.md, the 13 audit reports in .overhaul/audit/, repo at overhaul/multiplatform-v2 (HEAD 99c4edf), PeriFlow and VitFleet | design/MASTER-PLAN.md:3-9 | orchestrator | revision 2 (2026-10-09) |
| MP-0.conv | Conventions: R = D:/projects/tools/android-base (main checkout, integration branch, orchestrator only); OV = D:/projects/tools/.overhaul; W = OV/wt/<WP> on wp/<WP>; L = OV/bin/locked; O = OV/out/<WP>; P = OV/perf/<WP>; H = OV/handoff/<WP>.md; A/, M/, I/ = templates/android / multiplatform / ios; PKG = com/base/app; PF, VF = PeriFlow, VitFleet paths | design/MASTER-PLAN.md:13-24 | all | convention (paths already Windows in this file) |
| MP-0.lock | Every Gradle/device command uses the lock wrapper and exported ANDROID_HOME (C:/Users/crazyhusir/AppData/Local/Android/Sdk); $L gradle ./gradlew <tasks> --console=plain; $L adb adb -s 9a7c74f4 <args>; both locks ALWAYS adb first then gradle | design/MASTER-PLAN.md:26-34 | all | convention |
| MP-0.background | locked is exec flock with no timeout, agent tool calls capped at 10 minutes, a killed client cancels the daemon build half-way: every command that can run longer than ~2 minutes (incl. lock wait) runs in the background with output in $O/logs/<step>.log and the agent polls; every acceptance block is a script $O/accept.sh (set -euo pipefail, last line echo ACCEPT OK) | design/MASTER-PLAN.md:36-47 | all | convention |
| MP-0.appid | Device work uses an application id unique to the package (create_project.py --spec <fixture> --set package_name=com.example.<wp>.<name>; e.g. com.example.wp05.everything, dev flavour com.example.wp05.everything.dev) | design/MASTER-PLAN.md:49-51 | all | convention |
| MP-1.1 | Decision 1 Layout: template/ -> templates/android/; new templates/multiplatform/ and templates/ios/; one stdlib-only Python engine (generator/genkit/core) with plugin per target; template.toml, features/<key>.toml, presets.toml; shared tokens/*.json emitted into Kotlin, Swift, website | design/MASTER-PLAN.md:55-59 | WP00, WP01 | decision |

## Part 15: What this inventory did not cover (so nothing is assumed)

- Not in my assignment and not read: HANDOVER.md sections 0-2.3 and 5-9 (including 1.7 "decisions only the user can make", 5.x orchestration, 6.x rules, 8 first 30 minutes), CHAT-HISTORY.md, USER-MESSAGES.md, MANIFEST.md, WIPE-READINESS.md, RESUME-ON-WINDOWS.md, PUSHED.md, BRANCH-README.md, handover/tools/*, and design/wp/WP00.md ... WP14.md (these hold each package's own acceptance scripts and quick subsets; MASTER-PLAN B.4 only summarises them).
- MASTER-PLAN Part A was itemised by subsection (A1.1-A12.8) rather than line by line; B.4 summaries and the A12/B.1 acceptance lines are itemised in full.
- Row counts: the file holds 1,170 inventory rows (Parts 1-14). The structured result returned to the script is a condensed index of about 310 of them: goals, requirements, definitions of done, gates, checkpoints, plan steps, merge steps, per-requirement acceptance checks, user decisions, gate-process steps, Gradle matrix rows and the document differences. STATE-, APPX-, OWN-, RISK-, RULE-, PKG-, DEP-, CRIT-, ETA-, NUM- and the other rows exist only in this file.
